=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
There is a grid of N^2 squares with N rows and N columns.
Let (i,j) denote the square at the i-th row from the top (1\leq i\leq N) and j-th column from the left (1\leq j\leq N).
Each square is either empty or has a piece placed on it.
There are M pieces placed on the grid, and the k-th (1\leq k\leq M) piece is placed on square (a_k,b_k).
You want to place your piece on an empty square in such a way that it cannot be captured by any of the existing pieces.
A piece placed on square (i,j) can capture pieces that satisfy any of the following conditions:

- Placed in row i
- Placed in column j
- Placed on any square (a,b)\ (1\leq a\leq N,1\leq b\leq N) where i+j=a+b
- Placed on any square (a,b)\ (1\leq a\leq N,1\leq b\leq N) where i-j=a-b

For example, a piece placed on square (4,4) can capture pieces placed on the squares shown in blue in the following figure:

How many squares can you place your piece on?

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N M
a_1 b_1
a_2 b_2
\vdots
a_M b_M

Output

Print the number of empty squares where you can place your piece without it being captured by any existing pieces.

Constraints


- 1\leq N\leq10^9
- 1\leq M\leq10^3
- 1\leq a_k\leq N,1\leq b_k\leq N\ (1\leq k\leq M)
- (a_k,b_k)\neq(a_l,b_l)\ (1\leq k\lt l\leq M)
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

8 6
1 4
2 1
3 8
4 5
5 2
8 3

Sample Output 1

2

The existing pieces can capture pieces placed on the squares shown in blue in the following figure:

Therefore, you can place your piece on only two squares: squares (6,6) and (7,7).

Sample Input 2

1000000000 1
1 1

Sample Output 2

999999997000000002

Out of 10^{18} squares, the squares that cannot be used are: squares in row 1, squares in column 1, and squares (1,1), (2,2), \ldots, (10^9,10^9), totaling 3\times10^9-2 squares.
Note that the answer may be 2^{32} or greater.

Sample Input 3

20 10
1 4
7 11
7 15
8 10
11 6
12 5
13 1
15 2
20 10
20 15

Sample Output 3

77

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Now, potential performance and implementation details.

Input:
import sys
data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
N=data[0]; M=data[1]; then pairs.
Build sets:
rows=set(); cols=set(); diag=set(); anti=set()
for idx in range(2, 2+2*M, 2):
 a=data[idx]; b=data[idx+1]; rows.add(a); cols.add(b); diag.add(a-b); anti.add(a+b)

Then compute lengths:
def diag_len(d): return N - abs(d)
def anti_len(s): return min(s-1, 2*N+1-s)
sing = len(rows)*N + len(cols)*N + sum(N-abs(d) for d in diag) + sum(min(s-1, 2*N+1-s) for s in anti)

Pair counts:
pair_rc = len(rows)*len(cols)
pair_rd = 0
for r in rows:
 for d in diag:
   j = r - d
   if 1 <= j <= N: pair_rd += 1
pair_rs = 0
for r in rows:
 for s in anti:
   j = s - r
   if 1 <= j <= N: pair_rs += 1
pair_cd = 0
for c in cols:
 for d in diag:
   i = c + d
   if 1 <= i <= N: pair_cd += 1
pair_cs = 0
for c in cols:
 for s in anti:
   i = s - c
   if 1 <= i <= N: pair_cs += 1
pair_ds = 0
for d in diag:
 for s in anti:
   if (d + s) % 2 == 0:
      i = (d + s)//2
      j = (s - d)//2
      if 1 <= i <= N and 1 <= j <= N: pair_ds += 1

Triples:
triple_rcd = 0
for r in rows:
 for c in cols:
   if r - c in diag: triple_rcd += 1
triple_rcs = 0
for r in rows:
 for c in cols:
   if r + c in anti: triple_rcs += 1
triple_rds = 0
for d in diag:
 for s in anti:
   if (d+s)%2==0:
     r=(d+s)//2
     j=(s-d)//2
     if r in rows and 1 <= j <= N: triple_rds += 1
triple_cds = 0
for d in diag:
 for s in anti:
   if (s-d)%2==0:
     c=(s-d)//2
     i=(d+s)//2? Wait for Cols∩Diags∩Antis: cell at column c, main diag d => row i = c + d. Anti s: i+j = (c+d)+c = 2c+d = s => 2c = s-d => c=(s-d)//2. i = c+d = (s-d)//2 + d = (s+d)//2. Need i in [1,N], c in cols. So condition (s-d) even, c=(s-d)//2, i=(s+d)//2. Alternatively iterate d,s and check c in cols and i valid. Need parity of s-d same as s+d (same parity), so if (s-d)%2==0 then (s+d)%2==0 automatically. Use c=(s-d)//2; i=(s+d)//2.
     if c in cols and 1 <= i <= N: triple_cds += 1
Quad:
quad = 0
for r in rows:
 for c in cols:
   if (r - c in diag) and (r + c in anti): quad += 1

Then union = sing - (pair_rc+pair_rd+pair_rs+pair_cd+pair_cs+pair_ds) + (triple_rcd+triple_rcs+triple_rds+triple_cds) - quad
ans = N*N - union

Implementation plan:
def solve():
    import sys
    it = iter(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = next(it); M = next(it)
    rows=set(); cols=set(); diag=set(); anti=set()
    for _ in range(M):
        a=next(it); b=next(it)
        rows.add(a); cols.add(b); diag.add(a-b); anti.add(a+b)
    R=list(rows); C=list(cols); D=list(diag); S=list(anti)
    # maybe sort not needed.
    single = len(R)*N + len(C)*N
    single += sum(N - abs(d) for d in D)
    single += sum(min(s-1, 2*N+1-s) for s in S)

    pair_rc = len(R)*len(C)
    pair_rd=0
    for r in R:
        for d in D:
            j = r - d
            if 1 <= j <= N:
                pair_rd += 1
    pair_rs=0
    for r in R:
        for s in S:
            j = s - r
            if 1 <= j <= N:
                pair_rs += 1
    pair_cd=0
    for c in C:
        for d in D:
            i = c + d
            if 1 <= i <= N:
                pair_cd += 1
    pair_cs=0
    for c in C:
        for s in S:
            i = s - c
            if 1 <= i <= N:
                pair_cs += 1
    pair_ds=0
    for d in D:
        for s in S:
            if ((d + s) & 1) == 0:
                i = (d + s)//2
                j = (s - d)//2
                if 1 <= i <= N and 1 <= j <= N:
                    pair_ds += 1

    triple_rcd=0
    for r in R:
        for c in C:
            if r - c in diag:
                triple_rcd += 1
    triple_rcs=0
    for r in R:
        for c in C:
            if r + c in anti:
                triple_rcs += 1
    # Could combine R×C loop for rcd, rcs, quad to reduce loops. Yes, do one loop over R×C:
    triple_rcd=0; triple_rcs=0; quad=0
    for r in R:
        for c in C:
            d = r - c; s = r + c
            if d in diag: triple_rcd += 1
            if s in anti: triple_rcs += 1
            if d in diag and s in anti: quad += 1
    # This is 1e6 loops, okay. Note quad counts cells with both conditions. Good.

    triple_rds=0
    for d in D:
        for s in S:
            if ((d + s) & 1) == 0:
                r = (d + s)//2
                j = (s - d)//2
                if r in rows and 1 <= j <= N:
                    triple_rds += 1
    triple_cds=0
    for d in D:
        for s in S:
            # parity condition: s-d even. If d+s even same. Use if ((s - d) & 1)==0:
            if ((s - d) & 1) == 0:
                c = (s - d)//2
                i = (s + d)//2
                if c in cols and 1 <= i <= N:
                    triple_cds += 1

    pairs = pair_rc + pair_rd + pair_rs + pair_cd + pair_cs + pair_ds
    triples = triple_rcd + triple_rcs + triple_rds + triple_cds
    union = single - pairs + triples - quad
    ans = N*N - union
    print(ans)

Let's write final code cleanly:

import sys

def solve():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N = data[0]
    M = data[1]
    rows = set()
    cols = set()
    diag = set()
    anti = set()
    idx = 2
    for _ in range(M):
        a = data[idx]; b = data[idx+1]; idx += 2
        rows.add(a)
        cols.add(b)
        diag.add(a - b)
        anti.add(a + b)

    R = list(rows)
    C = list(cols)
    D = list(diag)
    S = list(anti)

    single = len(R) * N + len(C) * N
    for d in D:
        single += N - abs(d)
    for s in S:
        single += min(s - 1, 2 * N + 1 - s)

    pair_rc = len(R) * len(C)

    pair_rd = 0
    for r in R:
        for d in D:
            j = r - d
            if 1 <= j <= N:
                pair_rd += 1

    pair_rs = 0
    for r in R:
        for s in S:
            j = s - r
            if 1 <= j <= N:
                pair_rs += 1

    pair_cd = 0
    for c in C:
        for d in D:
            i = c + d
            if 1 <= i <= N:
                pair_cd += 1

    pair_cs = 0
    for c in C:
        for s in S:
            i = s - c
            if 1 <= i <= N:
                pair_cs += 1

    pair_ds = 0
    triple_rds = 0
    triple_cds = 0
    for d in D:
        for s in S:
            if ((d + s) & 1) == 0:
                i = (d + s) // 2
                j = (s - d) // 2
                if 1 <= i <= N and 1 <= j <= N:
                    pair_ds += 1
                    if i in rows:
                        triple_rds += 1
                    if j in cols:
                        triple_cds += 1

    triple_rcd = 0
    triple_rcs = 0
    quad = 0
    for r in R:
        for c in C:
            d = r - c
            s = r + c
            in_d = d in diag
            in_s = s in anti
            if in_d:
                triple_rcd += 1
            if in_s:
                triple_rcs += 1
            if in_d and in_s:
                quad += 1

    pairs = pair_rc + pair_rd + pair_rs + pair_cd + pair_cs + pair_ds
    triples = triple_rcd + triple_rcs + triple_rds + triple_cds
    union = single - pairs + triples - quad
    ans = N * N - union
    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    solve()

Before final, let's double-check all formulas by deriving from set theory one more time.

Let A = rows union, B = cols union, C = main diag union, D = anti diag union.
|A| = |R|N.
|B| = |C|N.
|C| = sum_{d∈D} len(d).
|D| = sum_{s∈S} len(s).

Pair intersections:
|A∩B| = |R||C|.
|A∩C| = # {(r,d)∈R×D : 1≤r-d≤N}. Because row r and main diagonal d intersect at (r, r-d). If j in range. Good.
|A∩D| = # {(r,s)∈R×S : 1≤s-r≤N}.
|B∩C| = # {(c,d)∈C×D : 1≤c+d≤N}. Because column c and main diagonal d: i=c+d. Good.
|B∩D| = # {(c,s)∈C×S : 1≤s-c≤N}.
|C∩D| = # {(d,s)∈D×S : d+s even, 1≤(d+s)/2≤N, 1≤(s-d)/2≤N}. Good.

Triple:
|A∩B∩C| = # {(r,c)∈R×C : r-c∈D}. Because cell at row r col c has main diag r-c. Good.
|A∩B∩D| = # {(r,c)∈R×C : r+c∈S}.
|A∩C∩D| = # {(d,s)∈D×S : intersection point has row in R and inside}. We use i=(d+s)/2, j=(s-d)/2; if i∈R and j valid. Good.
|B∩C∩D| = # {(d,s)∈D×S : intersection point has col in C and inside}. We use j∈C and i valid. Good.

Quad:
|A∩B∩C∩D| = # {(r,c)∈R×C : r-c∈D and r+c∈S}. Good.

Now, let's think about if in triple A∩C∩D, the intersection of row r, main diagonal d, anti s is a cell if row r equals i=(d+s)/2. We count if i∈R. But what if there is a row r selected and main diagonal d selected and anti s selected, but the cell (r, r-d) has anti s = r + (r-d) = 2r - d. Setting equal s gives 2r = d+s. Yes. So i=(d+s)/2. Good. j=(s-d)/2. Good.

Triple B∩C∩D: column c, main d, anti s: cell (c+d, c). Anti sum = c+d+c = 2c+d = s => 2c = s-d. So c=(s-d)/2. Row i=(s+d)/2. Good.

Now, let's think about if in combined D×S loop, for triple_rds we count if `i in rows` but what if `i` is not integer? We're inside parity. Good. What if `i` is valid row but `j` invalid? We only count if pair_ds valid (j valid). Good.
For triple_cds, count if `j in cols` and pair_ds valid (i valid). Good.

Now, let's think about if there is any issue with "anti length" if s-1 maybe >N for s>N+1, but min with 2N+1-s. Good.

Now, let's consider if there is any issue with "pair_ds" if d+s even but i/j maybe not in [1,N]; skip
</think>

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys

def solve():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return

    N = data[0]
    M = data[1]

    rows = set()
    cols = set()
    diag = set()
    anti = set()

    idx = 2
    for _ in range(M):
        a = data[idx]
        b = data[idx + 1]
        idx += 2
        rows.add(a)
        cols.add(b)
        diag.add(a - b)
        anti.add(a + b)

    R = list(rows)
    C = list(cols)
    D = list(diag)
    S = list(anti)

    # Single lines
    single = len(R) * N + len(C) * N
    for d in D:
        single += N - abs(d)
    for s in S:
        single += min(s - 1, 2 * N + 1 - s)

    # Pair intersections
    pair_rc = len(R) * len(C)

    pair_rd = 0
    for r in R:
        for d in D:
            j = r - d
            if 1 <= j <= N:
                pair_rd += 1

    pair_rs = 0
    for r in R:
        for s in S:
            j = s - r
            if 1 <= j <= N:
                pair_rs += 1

    pair_cd = 0
    for c in C:
        for d in D:
            i = c + d
            if 1 <= i <= N:
                pair_cd += 1

    pair_cs = 0
    for c in C:
        for s in S:
            i = s - c
            if 1 <= i <= N:
                pair_cs += 1

    pair_ds = 0
    triple_rds = 0
    triple_cds = 0

    for d in D:
        for s in S:
            if ((d + s) & 1) == 0:
                i = (d + s) // 2
                j = (s - d) // 2
                if 1 <= i <= N and 1 <= j <= N:
                    pair_ds += 1
                    if i in rows:
                        triple_rds += 1
                    if j in cols:
                        triple_cds += 1

    # Triple intersections and quadruple intersection
    triple_rcd = 0
    triple_rcs = 0
    quad = 0

    for r in R:
        for c in C:
            d = r - c
            s = r + c
            in_d = d in diag
            in_s = s in anti
            if in_d:
                triple_rcd += 1
            if in_s:
                triple_rcs += 1
            if in_d and in_s:
                quad += 1

    pairs = pair_rc + pair_rd + pair_rs + pair_cd + pair_cs + pair_ds
    triples = triple_rcd + triple_rcs + triple_rds + triple_cds

    union = single - pairs + triples - quad
    ans = N * N - union
    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    solve()
```
